10 月 PMI 数据点评

制造业需求整体回落至收缩区间   
制造业景气度回落至收缩区间。制造业需求整体回落至收缩区间。尽管 10
月建筑业景气度有所回落， 但10 月专用设备制造业长期盈利能力领先于其他
制造业子行业，四季度固定资产投资仍有望发力。 
 制造业景气度回落至收缩区间。10 月制造业 PMI 指数为 49.3%， 环比 （9
月，下同）下降 0.8 个百分点，制造业景气度回落至收缩区间。 
 分产业看，10 月高技术制造业 PMI 为 51.3%，环比下滑 1.2 个百分点，
但仍高于制造业总体水平，且维持在较高景气度区间，仍是当前制造业
景气度回升的重要驱动；原材料行业 PMI 为 47.3%，在收缩区间有所下
探；10 月消费品行业 PMI 为 48.8%，环比下滑 1.9 个百分点，行业景气
度回落至收缩区间，中秋、国庆假期因素消退后，消费行业景气度或出
现自然回落。 
10 月装备制造业 PMI 为 50.6%，较 9 月小幅下滑 0.4 个百分点，但仍然
维持在扩张区间； 尽管10 月建筑业 PMI 再度回落至收缩区间， 但装备制
造业 PMI 仍表现出一定韧性，我们认为，四季度扩内需政策仍有发力空
间，或对相关产业景气度有所带动。 
 制造业需求整体回落至收缩区间。着眼重要细分项， 10 月新订单指数
49.4%，环比下滑 1.1 个百分点；10 月新出口订单指数环比回落 1.3 个百
分点，当月值为 48.7%；10 月产成品库存指数 47.5%， 环比下滑0.1 个百
分点；制造业需求整体回落至收缩区间。 
10 月生产指数 49.8%，环比回落 1.9 个百分点；原材料库存指数 48.4%，
环比上升 0.2 个百分点； 从业人员指数48.4 %， 环比9 月持平； 供货商配
送时间指数 50.2%，环比上升 0.1 个百分点。企业预期方面，制造业生产
经营活动预期指数 53.4%，环比回落 0.4 个百分点，制造业经营预期维持
在较高景气度区间。从生产相关的细分指标看，月内在手订单和采购指
数环比分别下滑 1.9 和 2.1 个百分点。 
10 月制造业生产指数环比下降， 制造业采购备货、 在手订单均环比下降，
或在一定程度上受到假期因素影响，国庆假期时间较长，工作日减少或
对企业生产有所影响。 
 10 月非制造业 PMI 指数回升至扩张区间。10 月非制造业 PMI（商务活动）
指数为 49.7%， 环比下滑0.5 个百分点。 从需求端看，10 月新订单指数为
44.4%，环比下滑 2.1 个百分点。外需方面，新出口订单指数为 46.5%，
环比下滑 2.0 个百分点。10 月非制造业从业人员指数为 46.0%， 环比上月
走高 0.1 个百分点，但仍处于较深的收缩区间。 供应商配送时间指数
50.7%，环比回落 0.6 个百分点。 
 风险提示：海外大宗商品价格震荡；地缘关系的不确定性；政策效果不
及预期。

### Page 2

2026 年 11 月 1 日 10 月 PMI 数据点评 2

制造业需求整体回落至收缩区间 
制造业景气度回落至收缩区间。10 月制造业 PMI 指数为 49.3%，环比（9 月，下同）下降 0.8 个百
分点，制造业景气度回落至收缩区间。 
分产业看，10 月高技术制造业 PMI 为 51.3%，环比下滑 1.2 个百分点，但仍高于制造业总体水平，
且维持在较高景气度区间，仍是当前制造业景气度回升的重要驱动；原材料行业 PMI 为 47.3%，在
收缩区间有所下探；10 月消费品行业 PMI 为 48.8%，环比下滑 1.9 个百分点，行业景气度回落至收
缩区间，中秋、国庆假期因素消退后，消费行业景气度或出现自然回落。 
10 月装备制造业 PMI 为 50.6%，较 9 月小幅下滑 0.4 个百分点，但仍然维持在扩张区间；尽管 10
月建筑业 PMI 再度回落至收缩区间，但装备制造业 PMI 仍表现出一定韧性，我们认为，四季度扩内
需政策仍有发力空间，或对相关产业景气度有所带动。 
制造业需求整体回落至收缩区间。着眼重要细分项，10 月新订单指数 49.4%，环比下滑 1.1 个百分
点；10 月新出口订单指数环比回落 1.3 个百分点，当月值为 48.7%；10 月产成品库存指数 47.5%，
环比下滑 0.1 个百分点；制造业需求整体回落至收缩区间。 
10 月生产指数 49.8%，环比回落 1.9 个百分点；原材料库存指数48.4%，环比上升 0.2 个百分点；从
业人员指数 48.4 %，环比 9 月持平；供货商配送时间指数50.2%，环比上升 0.1 个百分点。企业预期
方面，制造业生产经营活动预期指数53.4%，环比回落 0.4 个百分点， 制造业经营预期维持在较高景
气度区间 。从生产相关的细分指标看 ， 月内在手订单和采购指数环比分别下滑 1.9 和 2.1 个百分点。 
10 月制造业生产指数环比下降，制造业采购备货、在手订单均环比下降，或在一定程度上受到假期
因素影响，国庆假期时间较长，工作日减少或对企业生产有所影响。

图表 1. 制造业 PMI 与历史同期相比（%）

资料来源：wind，中银证券

价格指数方面 ，10 月主要原材料购进价格指数 56.8 %,环比回落 4.0 百分点 ，但仍处于较高景气区间；
制造业出厂价格指数也维持在扩张区间，环比下滑 2.3 个百分点， 当月值为51.7%；主要原材料购进
价格指数水平仍然高于出厂价格指数，上游成本端或仍是 10 月价格指数的主要支撑。 
重点行业盈利能力方面，尽管 10 月建筑业景气度有所回落，但 10 月专用设备制造业长期盈利能力
领先于其他制造业子行业，四季度固定资产投资仍有望发力；同期汽车制造业短期、长期盈利能力
也在各行业中较为领先，汽车产业链盈利能力延续改善；计算机通信电子设备及仪器仪表制造业短
期盈利能力较为领先，长期盈利能力在各行业中处于中等偏上位置；此外，有色金属冶炼及压延加
工业长期盈利也在各行业中较为领先。

大型制造企业支撑继续凸显。10 月大型企业 PMI 为 50.2%，较 9 月回落 0.4 个百分点，但仍处于扩
张区间；中、小型制造企业 PMI 分别为 48.4%和 47.5%，环比分别回落 1.3 和 1.4 个百分点，中小企
业景气度仍处于收缩区间。

10 月非制造业 PMI 指数回升至扩张区间 
10 月非制造业 PMI 指数回升至扩张区间。10 月非制造业 PMI（商务活动）指数为 49.7%，环比下滑
0.5 个百分点。从需求端看，10 月新订单指数为 44.4%，环比下滑 2.1 个百分点。外需方面，新出口
订单指数为 46.5%，环比下滑 2.0 个百分点。10 月非制造业从业人员指数为 46.0%，环比上月走高
0.1 个百分点，但仍处于较深的收缩区间。供应商配送时间指数 50.7%，环比回落 0.6 个百分点。

图表 3. 非制造业 PMI 与历史同期相比（%）

### Page 4

2026 年 11 月 1 日 10 月 PMI 数据点评 4

10 月建筑业景气度再度回落至收缩区间。建筑业 PMI 指数 48.0%，环比回落 2.3 个百分点，建筑业
景气度再度回落至收缩区间。10 月新订单指数 40.0%， 环比下滑 5.7 个百分点 。 从业人员指数42.0%，
环比下滑 2.3 个百分点。此外，业务活动预期指数52.3%，环比回落 3.1 个百分点，10 月建筑业多个
分项指标有所下滑，新订单、从业人员指数及业务活动预期均有下降。 
10 月服务业景气指数持平于临界点。10 月服务业 PMI 指数 50.0%，环比回落 0.2 个百分点，服务业
景气指数持平于临界点。新订单指数 45.2%，环比回落 1.5 个百分点；销售价格指数环比回落 1.0 个
百分点，至 49.6%；从业人员指数环比上升 0.5 个百分点，为 46.7%。

披露声明 
本报告准确表述了证券分析师的个人观点。该证券分析师声明，本人未在公司内、外部机构兼任有损本人独立性与客
观性的其他职务，没有担任本报告评论的上市公司的董事、监事或高级管理人员；也不拥有与该上市公司有关的任何
财务权益；本报告评论的上市公司或其它第三方都没有或没有承诺向本人提供与本报告有关的任何补偿或其它利益。 
中银国际证券股份有限公司同时声明，将通过公司网站披露本公司授权公众媒体及其他机构刊载或者转发证券研究报
告有关情况。 如有投资者于未经授权的公众媒体看到或从其他机构获得本研究报告的， 请慎重使用所获得的研究报告，
以防止被误导，中银国际证券股份有限公司不对其报告理解和使用承担任何责任。 
评级体系说明 
以报告发布日后公司股价/行业指数涨跌幅相对同期相关市场指数的涨跌幅的表现为基准： 
公司投资评级： 
买    入：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 20%以上； 
增    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内超越基准指数 10%-20%； 
中    性：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数变动幅度在-10%-10%之间； 
减    持：预计该公司股价在未来 6-12 个月内相对基准指数跌幅在 10%以上； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

行业投资评级： 
强于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现强于基准指数； 
中    性：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现基本与基准指数持平； 
弱于大市：预计该行业指数在未来 6-12 个月内表现弱于基准指数； 
未有评级：因无法获取必要的资料或者其他原因，未能给出明确的投资评级。

沪深市场基准指数为沪深 300 指数；新三板市场基准指数为三板成指或三板做市指数；香港市场基准指数为恒生指数
或恒生中国企业指数；美股市场基准指数为纳斯达克综合指数或标普500 指数。

### Page 1

M
Update
Unimicron  | Asia Pacific
Key ABF substrate customer
announces capacity reservation
agreements
What's new? According to  a press release on October 21, at GTC Berlin NVIDIA
(covered by Joseph Moore) named Unimicron as one of  three partners in its plan to
lift substrate capacity 2.5x over three years. A capacity reservation agreement
supports Unimicron’s KF (Kuangfu) plant expansion in Taiwan, with additions
expected from 2Q27 and full capacity by mid-2028.

Our read-through: We believe this implies robust multi-year, AI-driven substrate
demand. NVIDIA’s capacity reservation likely covers Unimicron's 2Q27 KF Plant 2
step flagged in July (see our earnings call takeaways), which could lower the
financial risk of overcapacity as Unimicron deploys its NT$53.7bn 2026 capex
budget. Stay OW with a price target of NT$1,775.

### Page 2

M
Update
2
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 163: 261030_ubs_MTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/261030_ubs_MTK.pdf

### Page 1

ab 30 October 2026
Global Research
Key Call: MediaTek Inc.
Raising TPU guidance with stronger execution
TPU v9 on track to ramp from early 2028
Amid market skepticism on TPU execution, MediaTek delivered better outlook for Q4
and Cloud ASICs, as we previewed. Management raised TPU sales guidance from >US
$2bn to US$2.5bn for 2026, and from US$12-16bn to US$16bn for 2027. This reflects
the steady Google demand for v8t for training, critical to accelerate the new Gemini LLM

development. Importantly, for TPU v9, MediaTek re-affirmed it is on track for high
volume manufacturing in Q128, which clarified  recent noises around delayed tape out.
Our latest understanding is there may be two versions of MediaTek’s TPU v9: the first
version with 336G SerDes should be tape out soon in Nov 2026. There’s a second tape
out which is on track for delivery in Feb 2027. The second version features flexible
SerDes switch between 224G/336G for greater supply flexibility for the whole system.

Conclusion is, we think MediaTek is on good track to scale TPU  sales to our estimates of
US$44bn/53bn in 2028/29  with upside. See Figure 1U B S  f o r e c a s t s  f o r   M e d i a T e k ' s  T P U  b u s i n e s s for our TPU financial analysis.
Thoughts on the second Cloud ASIC client
MediaTek announced the 2nd Cloud ASIC client, which should contribute to revenue in
2028. MediaTek clarified it’s not a Chinese customer. We believe there are 2 higher

possibilities as what we flagged recently. 1) Tesla’s AI6 using Intel 14A, produced at
Terafab ramping in H228. Potential revenue opportunity on full year basis could be ~US
$2bn if MediaTek could ramp 1m units. Margin profile could be lower than TPU's 42%
GM given simpler design. 2) SpaceX/xAI's Dojo3. MediaTek should be partnering with
Nvidia to offer NVLink Fusion for faster time to market. Question is SpaceX/xAI's own
engineering resource and capability for Dojo3 after team reshuffle. If secured, our initial

estimate is revenue opportunity a year could be US$4-9bn, assuming good volume.
Margin profile may be similar or higher than TPU, in our view.
Meaningful operating margin expansion ahead
Q4 sales are guided to grow 28% QoQ at the mid point, beating consensus of 25%,
even with higher base from Q3. GM is guided to edge down to 45.0% at mid point, with
OPM meaningfully rising to 19.0% from 16.1% in Q3. As MediaTek starts ramping TPU
substantially into 2027, the key focus for profitability should shift from GM to OPM due

to different business model vs consumer electronics, and our sense is the market still
under appreciates the structural shift and the stronger earnings power. We forecast
MediaTek's OPM to rise to 25.2%/30.9% in 2027/28 vs consensus of 21.8%/24.2%.
Our 2027/28E EPS estimates are 23%/29% above consensus pre earnings.
Valuation: maintain Key Call Buy and target of NT$7,300
Among our coverage, MediaTek remains the one that we believe the market most

underestimates its execution and opportunity in Cloud AI. We raise 2026E EPS by 5%
and fine tune 2027/28E EPS by 1-2%. Maintain Key Call Buy. Our target of NT$7,300 is
based on 27x 2027-28E PE.

Company Description
MediaTek is a leading integrated circuit (IC) design company in Taiwan, focusing on solutions
from the edge to the cloud, applications for its product offerings range from smartphones,
smart homes and AI PCs to high-performance computing, automotive, and AI data centers.

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
Our price target for MediaTek is based on a target PE methodology.
Fabless integrated circuit (IC) design houses face a variety of uncertainties, including rapidly
changing technology, end-market demand, competition, employee turnover and foundry
wafer costs. They are also vulnerable to legal disputes over intellectual property rights. In
addition, MediaTek is exposed to potential price competition in handset SoC, TV SoC, etc.

Downside risks include slower-than-expected market share gains and a weaker-than-
expected demand recovery.

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

## Document 185: 3680家登 台新

Source PDF: all report pdf/3680家登 台新.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-27
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

本刊載之報告為台新投顧於特定日期之分析，已力求陳述內容之可靠性，純屬研究性質，僅作參考，使用者應明瞭內容之時效性，
審慎考量投資風險，並就投資結果自行負責。報告著作權屬台新投顧所有，禁止任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。 
台新證券投資顧問股份有限公司 台北市仁愛路四段 118 號 16 樓(02)5589-9558  (113) 金管投顧新字第 021 號 
公司拜訪快報

家登(3680)：EUV POD、中國 FOUP 雙軌成長，展望正向 
建議：維持買進

一、3Q26 營收創高，EUV POD 放量與產品組合優化推
升獲利 
家登 3Q26 營收 27.1 億元 ，+16.9% QoQ，+62.9% YOY，
受惠 EUV POD 及 FOUP 出貨同步成長，營運規模持續
擴大，除台灣主要客戶需求穩健外，美系大客戶出貨量
亦大幅增加，帶動高單價產品營收貢獻，另中國市場
FOUP 需求回溫 ， 多個客戶同步拉貨挹注營收 。 考量EUV 
POD 及中國FOUP 均為影響公司獲利結構的重要產品，
預期在出貨規模擴大、產品組合維持有利的情況下，家
登 3Q26 營收與獲利可望同步展現強勁成長。在產能利
用率提升、加上產品組合優化下，預估毛利率上升至
43.9%， 季增1.2%、 年增4.8%， 營業利益達5.3 億元，
營業利益率 19.7%，季增 2.4%、年增 10.6%，稅後歸
屬母公司淨利 4.2 億元，EPS 4.41 元。 
二、4Q26 營運維持高檔 
展望 4Q26，EUV POD 及 FOUP 需求延續，加上台灣
大客戶先進製程產能擴張，帶動載具需求續，預估家登
4Q26 營收 27.7 億元，+2.3% QoQ，+22.0% YoY。預
期稼動率及生產效率提升將改善毛利率，惟考量海外布
局、折舊攤提、人力擴編等因子下，以保守態度估計毛
利率略提升至 44.2%，季增 0.3 百分點，年增 0.9 百分
點 ， 不排除後續上修空間，預估營益率19.9%，季減 0.2
百分點 ， 年減增2.4 百分點 ， 歸屬母公司淨利4.2 億元，
EPS 為 4.35 元。 
三、先進製程擴產和中國 FOUP 回溫為公司成長雙動能 
家登的光罩載具以 EUV POD 為主要成長引擎，隨 AI
發展帶動先進製程光罩層數、客戶開案量及載具汰換需
求皆顯著增加，預期 EUV POD 用量穩健提升，就產業
端觀察 ， 台灣大客戶先進製程節點已佔其整體營收79%，
且投資金額、規模持續擴大，而 ASML 最新法說也顯示
EUV 設備需求強勁，為 EUV 供應鏈的中長期成長提供
支撐，另外，除台灣主要客戶需求穩健外，今年美系客
戶出貨量亦大幅增加，預期 2027 年延續有機成長。晶
圓載具方面，中國 FOUP 需求明顯回溫，且客戶組成較
過往更加多元，整體貢獻有望回到前波高峰。 
四、先進製程投資延續，2026-2027 年成長可期 
AI 發展推動先進製程進入一波新投資循環，ASML 近期
法說會表示，EUV 設備需求維持高檔，直至 2028 年都
供不應求，已有客戶針對 2029 年後訂單展開討論，而
台灣大客戶亦提及 2030 年將導入 High-NA EUV 進行量
產，我們認為 AI 相關需求不墜，可支持家登 EUV POD
中長期需求，惟實際營收貢獻仍視客戶開案、光罩使用
量及載具採購進度而定。獲利方面，內部效率提升與成
本反映有助改善部分產品利潤，預估 2026/2027 年營收
為 96.5/117.1 億元，+31.4%/21.3% YoY，EPS 
16.83/22.70 元，目前股價依 2026 年 EPS 推估，約位
於 30.5 倍本益比，維持買進評等，目標價615 元(35.0x 
3Q26-2Q27PER)。

2026/10/21 投資速報

營收走勢圖

研究員/張均壕 JUN_HAO1@uni-psg.com

研究部對拓凱未來營運看法轉趨正向 ，主要是考量：(1)占營收比
重最大的自行車業務在 2H26 出現改善，隨著主力客戶新品銷售表
現較佳，帶動訂單增加，預期 2027 年自行車業務有望止跌回溫。
(2)航太、安全帽及其他類業務接單保持強勁，其中航太受惠於航
空座椅需求增加，安全帽則有高階產品及新客戶訂單貢獻，加上精
密塑件業務持續擴張 ， 將有助於優化產品組合 。(3) 2Q26 受子公司
一次性費用及匯率等因素干擾，毛利率與獲利下滑，但隨下半年出
貨規模擴大、產品組合改善，預期 2H26 獲利能力將逐步回升。研
究部預估拓凱 2026 年 EPS 13.52 元(-9%YOY)，2027 年EPS 15.28
元(+13%YOY)。因公司營運重回成長軌道，且產品組合改善有利於
中長期獲利表現，故以 2027 年獲利為評價基礎，給予 12 倍本益
比，目標價 183 元，投資評等由中立調升至買進。

重點摘要：

1. 主力客戶新車種上市，自行車業務回溫 ： 
拓凱營收約有 50% 來自碳纖維自行車車架與組件，最大核心
客戶為全球知名自行車 品牌-Specialized。雖然公司的自行
車業務在1H26衰退近二成，但受惠主力客戶 年中新品上市並
在終端市場銷售亮眼 ， 客戶對拓凱積極追加車架 、 輪圈與前叉
等複材組件訂單 ， 帶動拓凱近期自行車業務顯著改善 。 在去年
下半年低基期與今年新車拉貨潮帶動下，預計 2026全年自行
車營收減幅將收斂至個位數（研究部估減少8%YoY）。2027年
隨客戶新品需求與庫存去化完畢，預 估自行車業務營收將能
回溫，成長3%YoY。

2. 安全帽、航太及其他類業務展望正向，成長動能強勁： 
自行車以外業務為拓凱營運成長的核心動能 。 其中 ， 拓凱的安
全帽業務近年出貨增加 ， 且產品結構大幅改善 ， 高階產品占比
已達2/3，預計帶動今年安全帽營收大幅增長30%YoY，且因引
進電子智能安全帽新客戶，預期明年該業務營收仍可成長
7%YoY。球拍業務方面，今年出貨暢旺，預期今年球拍營收能
創下歷史新高 ， 全年度球拍營收預計成長18%YoY。 不過 ， 因近
兩年基期拉高，明年看法較謹慎，預計小幅衰退5%YoY。航太
醫療業務方面 ， 航太接單展望樂觀 ， 預期今年該類別營收增長
14%YoY，且1Q27起新增飛機座椅訂單將正式投產 ， 預計明年該
類別營收將再有12%YoY的增速 。 另外 ， 歸類在其他類之子公司

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

新鴻洲部分，受惠自行車煞把手、電子TOF精密塑件等產品出
貨增加 ， 營運動能依舊偏強 ， 預計該業務2026年、2027年營收
分別成長23%、13%YoY。

3. 一次性負面因素淡化，3Q26毛利率與獲利將回升： 
回顧拓凱2Q26財報 ， 受提列子公司一次性費用的衝擊 ， 導致單
季毛利率下滑至31.3%(季減3個百分點，年減6.7個百分點)，
加上業外有逾6千萬元的匯損，拖累季度獲利表現，2Q26稅後
淨利2.67億元(-19%QoQ，-5%YoY)，2Q26EPS下滑至2.94元。然
而 ， 此費用屬於非經常性的短期會計調整 ， 本業高階複材競爭
力並未受損 。 下半年隨自行車業務改善 ， 拓凱3Q26合併營收回
升至27.16億元(+6%QoQ，+12%YoY)。 此外 ， 由於一次性因素淡
化 ， 加上高階安全帽 、 航太及其他類產品出貨增加 ， 產品組合
優化，研究部預估拓凱3Q26毛利率將回升至33.8%，營業利益
5.41億元(+37%QoQ，+13%YoY)，稅後淨利3.55億元(+33%QoQ，
+1%YoY)，3Q26EPS 3.91元，獲利將較第2季明顯改善。

4. 2026年營收近百億元，預估2026年EPS 13.52元： 
受惠於安全帽 、 球拍 、 航太及其他業務成長 ， 抵銷自行車業務
的衰退 ， 預期拓凱全年營收可望接近百億元 ， 研究部預估拓凱
2026年合併營收99.73億元(+8%YoY)。不過，由於2Q26受一次
性費用及匯率等因素干擾，今年度毛利率預估降至33.1%，營
業利益16.91億元(-2%YoY)，稅後淨利12.28億元(-9%YoY)，
EPS 13.52元。

5. 各業務成長帶動獲利回升，預估2027年EPS 15.28元： 
展望2027年 ， 隨自行車業務恢復成長 ， 航太及安全帽新訂單持
續貢獻，以及其他類業務穩健擴張，研究部預估 2027年營收
104.34億元(+5%YoY)， 毛利率回升至33.8%， 營業利益18.50億
元(+9%YoY)，稅後淨利13.88億元(+13%YoY)，EPS 15.28元。

6. 越南產能持續擴充，並積極佈局新應用商機 ： 
因應客戶分散生產基地與供應鏈調整需求，拓凱持續擴充越
南複合材料產品線 ， 目前除自行車前叉 、 車架等零組件外 ， 亦
逐步增加航空座椅零組件的生產，未來航太板材及球拍等產
品亦有機會導入越南製造 。 公司也已著手規劃越南二期廠房 ，
強化海外生產彈性 。 此外 ， 中科后里新廠除作為營運總部及研
發據點外 ， 也將支援新業務開發 。 初期鎖定人形機器人關鍵的
腰部與膝蓋複材結構件與客戶進行共同開發，同時也積極切
入農業與商用無人機 、 低軌衛星等先端應用 。 子公司新鴻洲方

面 ， 則運用精密射出及模具技術 ， 計畫開拓半導體與人形機器
人零件等應用 。 雖然新事業短期營收貢獻仍有限 ， 但隨產品開
發與客戶認證推進，長期有助於拓展複合材料及精密零組件
的應用領域。

評等  定義

供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議 。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見 ， 不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其 他 投
資 標 的 之 要 約 、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項 。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證 券 、 投
資 標 的 ， 或 文 中 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 投 資 策 略 ， 投 資 人 應 就 其 是 否 適 合 本 身 財 務 狀 況 與
投 資 條 件 ， 進 一 步 諮 詢 財 務 顧 問 的 意 見 。 本 投 資 報 告 之 內 容 取 材 自 據 信 為 可 靠 之 資
料 來 源 ， 但 概 不 以 明 示 或 默 示 的 方 式 ， 對 資 料 之 準 確 性 、 完 整 性 或 正 確 性 作 出 任 何
陳 述 或 保 證 。 本 投 資 報 告 載 述 意 見 進 行 更 改 與 撤 回 並 不 另 行 通 知 。 本 投 資 報 告 並 非
（且不應 解 釋 為 ）在 任 何 司 法 管 轄 區 內 ， 任 何 非 依 法 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 人 士 或
公 司 ， 為 於 該 管 轄 區 內 從 事 證 券 經 紀 或 交 易 之 遊 說 。 本 投 資 報 告 內 容 屬 統 一 投 顧 之
著 作 權 ， 嚴 禁 抄 襲 與 仿 造 。

## Document 230: [000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다실적에 수급을 더한다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[000660] SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다SK하이닉스(000660) 실적에 수급을 더한다실적에 수급을 더한다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
여전히 가격 상승 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 95.8조원, 영업이익 75.1조원으로 예상한다. 
이전 전망 대비 감소한 수준이다. 원/달러 환율 영향이다. 영업이익률은 78.4%로 전 
분기 대비 2.1%p 상승할 전망이다. DRAM 매출액은 73.1조원, 영업이익 
59.2조원으로 추정한다. DRAM ASP는 이전 전망 대비 상향 조정했다. 일부 제품 
가격 인상을 반영한 영향이다. Bit Growth는 이전 전망 수준을 유지했다. ASP 
상향이 수익성 개선에 미친 영향은 크지 않을 전망이다. NAND 매출액은 22.4조원, 
영업이익은 15.9조원으로 예상한다. 
Global Top 수준 주주환원 규모/자사주 살 게 더 있다 
SK하이닉스는 2026년 8월 19일 주주환원에 대한 새로운 정책을 제시했다. FCF에서 
CAPEX를 제외한 금액의 50% 이상을 주주환원에 사용한다는게 핵심이다. 2026년 
기준으로 최소 90조원 수준일 것으로 추정한다. 자사주 중심으로 주주환원을 계획 
중이고 이미 집행 중인 40조원 이외에 4분기 중에 추가로 자사주 매입이 진행될 
것으로 예상한다. 배당도 일부 집행이 되겠지만 자사주가 중심이 될 전망이다. 
흔들리지 말아야 할 이유는 많다 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 모델의 성장으로 메모리에 
대한 수요가 지속적으로 높아지고 있고, 이에 비해 공급 증가는 제한적인 상황이 
상당기간 지속될 것으로 전망하기 때문이다. 이로 인해 현재 수준의 DRAM, NAND 
가격에 변동성은 낮고, 높은 수익성이 유지되면서 안정적 현금흐름 창출이 가능할 
것으로 전망한다. 현재 주가는 지나치게 저평가되어 있는 국면에 있다고 판단한다. 
목표주가는 12개월 예상 EPS 446,092원에 PER 9 배를 적용한 4,000,000원을 
유지한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

## Document 246: [005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[005930] 삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다삼성전자(005930) 실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다실적은 좋지만, 주주환원은 아쉽다 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
아직 수요의 끝은 보이지 않는다 
AI 투자에 대한 우려의 목소리도 있지만 정작 부품을 공급하고 투자하는 쪽의 목소
리는 시간이 갈수록 더 커지고 있다. 공급 부족의 시점 매 분기 뒤로 밀려나고 있기 
때문이다. 28년까지 공급부족이라고 했던 메모리업체들은 이구동성으로 30년까지 공
급부족이 지속된다고 언급하고 있다. 이러한 변화는 실적에 고스란히 반영되고 있
다. 26년 3분기 메모리 영업이익만 108.5조원으로 추정한다. 4 분기에는 140조원에 
이를 것으로 전망한다. 
HBM4와 QLC는 차별화 포인트 
삼성전자는 HBM에서 입지가 좋았던 적이 없었다. 점유율, 수익성, 가동률, 제품 성
능, 고객 등 모든 항목에서 경쟁사 대비 열위에 있었다. HBM4는 모든 항목에서 입
지를 개선시킬 변수가 될 것으로 예상한다. 26년 3분기 물량은 경쟁사 수준일 것으
로 보이고 연간 물량도 유사한 수준일 전망이다. 27년에는 가격 인상과 가동률 상승
으로 수익성도 크게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. QLC는 TLC와 함께 AI 모델 진화의 
수혜를 받을 제품으로 빠르게 부상하고 있다. 메타의 Muse 영향이다. 접속자당 가
상 메모리를 NAND에 100GB를 할당하고 있기 때문이다.  
부족한 주주환원으로 투자 우선 순위에서 밀릴 듯 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 
변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, HBM 
시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 
판단하기 때문이다. 목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황
을 고려하면 적정 주가는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치
에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 
제한적일 것으로 판단하기 때문이다.

삼성전자 (005930)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기 매출액은 195.3조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.9% 증가한 195.3조원이다. VD/
가전을 제외한 주요 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 증가했다. DS, MX 모두 물량보다는 
가격 효과가 더 크게 작용한 것으로 분석된다. 사업부별로는  
1) DS 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 15.4% 증가한 147.1조원이다. 메모리
는 26년 2분기 대비 16.4% 증가했다. 26년 3분기 DRAM B/G(Bit Growth)
는 +2.0%, ASP는 +20.0%이다. NAND B/G는 +5.0%, ASP는 +18.0%이다. 
HBM 물량 증가는 HBM4가 주도했고, 그 이하 제품 비중은 하락한 것으로 
추정한다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 7.1% 증가한 8조원이다. 소형
은 해외 고객 및 국내 고객의 신제품 출시 영향으로 ASP 상승, 물량 증가 
효과가 동반되었고, 대형은 게이밍 모니터 비중을 확대했다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 13.4% 증가한 37.7조원이
다. MX는 15.5% 증가했다. 물량은 소폭 감소했고, ASP 상승 영향을 받았다. 
네트워크 사업부 매출액은 2분기 대비 60% 감소했다.  
4) VD/가전 사업부 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 4.1% 감소한 13.9조원이다. VD 
매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 6.5% 감소했다. 가전은 26년 2분기 대비 1.3% 
감소했다.

26년 3분기 영업이익은 107.4조원 
삼성전자의 26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 20% 증가한 107.4조원이다. DS,  
디스플레이와 MX는 2분기 대비 증가했고, VD/가전은 2분기 대비 감소했다. 사업부별
로는 
1) DS 사업부는 26년 2분기 대비 19.4% 증가한 106.5조원이다. 2분기 대비 영
업이익률은 소폭 상승했다. 메모리 영업이익은 2분기 대비 19.0% 증가했고, 
비메모리는 영업적자를 유지했다.  
2) 디스플레이 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 54.5% 증가한 1.1조원이다. 
중소형 신제품 효과로 수익성이 개선된 영향이다. 대형도 믹스 개선으로 수
익성이 개선되었다. 
3) MX/네트워크 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자폭이 감소했다. 물량
은 줄었지만 High End 비중 상승으로 ASP는 개선되었고, 원가 비중이 낮아
진 영향이다. 
4) VD/가전 사업부 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 적자 규모가 커졌다.

그림 2. 삼성전자 사업부별 분기별 영업이익 추이 및 전망

### Page 6

삼성전자 (005930)

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기 매출액은 226.5조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 16.0% 증가한 226.5조원으로 예상
한다. DS가 증가하고 나머지는 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS는 메모리가 성장을 주도할 것으로 기대한다. 가격과 물량 모두 3분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 전망한다. Context Server에 대한 수요가 증가하고, Personal Agent의 가상메
모리 설정으로 인해 NAND 수요가 증가하면서 QLC에 대한 수요 증가 속도가 빨라질 
전망이다. 이로 인해 NAND Bit Growth는 경쟁사와 차별화된 수준일 것으로 예상한
다.  
HBM 물량도 꾸준히 증가할 것으로 기대한다. 3 분기에도 HBM4를 중심으로 물량이 
크게 증가했다. HBM4는 SK하이닉스와 비슷한 물량일 것으로 예상한다. 아직 전체 
물량은 SK하이닉스가 더 많은 상황이고, HBM 내 HBM4 비중은 업계 최고 수준인 것
으로 판단한다. 26년까지는 HBM이 수익성에는 마이너스 요인이지만, 27년에는 
DRAM 수준의 영업이익이 가능할 것으로 기대한다.  
MX는 비수기 저가 라인 물량 조정으로 전체 물량은 감소하지만, 제품믹스는 개선되
어 판가는 3분기 대비 상승할 것으로 예상한다.

26년 4분기 영업이익은 138.2조원으로 예상 
삼성전자의 26년 4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 28.7% 증가한 138.2조원으로 예
상한다. DS 사업부를 제외하고 모두 3분기 대비 부진할 것으로 예상한다.  
DS 사업부 영업이익은 3분기 대비 30% 증가한 138.5조원으로 전망한다. DRAM, 
NAND 가격이 10% 초중반 수준으로 상승하고, 물량은 10% 중반 이상 증가하면서 3
분기 대비 영업이익률 개선을 기대하기 때문이다. HBM 물량 증가가 이익 개선에 미
치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상한다.  
디스플레이, MX는 물량 부진으로 3분기 대비 영업이익은 부진할 것으로 전망한다.

DS가 성장 주도 
DS만 보자

### Page 7

김운호  6915-5656

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
Check Point 
1. 모자란 듯한 주주환원 
26년 3분기 실적 발표에서 밝힌 주주환원 정책은 지난 8월의 내용과 다르지 않다. 3
분기 배당 30조원(주당 4,601원)을 확정한 정도이다. 잔여 배당 재원이 55~75조원으
로 구체화했고, 현금 배당과 자사주 매입을 병행하겠다고 밝혔다.  
자사주 매입 시 우선주 매입도 고려 대상이라고 언급한 것은 고무적이지만 27년부터 
새롭게 시작되는 주주환원 정책을 결정하기 위한 이사회 개최는 27년 1월에 열릴 것
으로 예고하였기에 연내 추가적인 주주환원 발표는 없을 것으로 판단한다.  
2. HBM4로 HBM 시장에서 입지 강화 
HBM4를 통해서 자신감을 회복하고 있는 것으로 판단한다. 양산 시점은 경쟁사 대비 
빨랐고, 3분기 물량은 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준인 것으로 추정한다. HBM3e가 부진했기 
때문에 전체 생산량에서 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 가장 높은 수준일 것으로 추정한다.  
26년 전체 물량도 경쟁사와 비슷한 수준이 될 것으로 전망한다. 삼성전자는 HBM 시
장 점유율을 DRAM 수준으로 맞추겠다는 목표를 3분기에 달성한 것으로 밝혔다.  
물량이 증가하면서 가동률도 빠르게 개선될 것으로 기대한다. 아직은 DRAM과 영업
이익률에서 큰 차이가 있지만 27년에는 인상된 가격, 높아진 수율, 가동률 개선으로 
DRAM과 영업이익률 차이를 좁힐 수 있을 것으로 기대한다.  
3. QLC NAND 시장 확대 
AI 컴퓨팅 확산은 NAND에게도 성장 기회이다. AI 모델들의 Context Window가 길어
져 KV Cache 메모리에 대한 수요가 커지면서 High Endurance TLC 수요가 급증하고 
있다. DRAM을 대체할 수준의 NAND가 필요하게 되었기 때문이다.  
이 뿐만 아니라 고용량 NAND에 대한 수요도 Personal Agent의 출현으로 급속히 증
가할 것으로 기대한다. 여기에는 QLC NAND가 주로 사용된다. 메타의 Muse는 접속
자 1인에 대해서 DRAM 8GB, NAND 100GB의 가상 메모리를 설정하고 운영한다.  
Agent AI의 확산은 스토리지에 대한 수요도 급격히 확대할 것으로 기대하고, 삼성전
자에게도 기회가 될 것으로 기대한다.  
26년 4분기 NAND Bit Growth는 삼성전자가 가장 높을 것으로 예상한다.

### Page 8

삼성전자 (005930)

8 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
투자의견 매수 유지, 목표주가 350,000원으로 하향 
삼성전자에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 이는 
1) 여러 변수에도 메모리 성장은 변화가 없을 것으로 전망하고, 
2) 메모리 수요는 장기적 성장 국면에 진입했고, 
3) HBM 시장 내 입지가 강화되고 있고, 
4) 현재 주가는 실적 대비 지나치게 저평가된 수준으로 판단하기 때문이다. 
목표주가는 350,000원으로 하향한다. 현재 수익성과 시장 상황을 고려하면 적정 주가
는 460,000원으로 판단하지만 주주환원 정책이 시장 기대치에 미치지 못하고, 경쟁사
와 비교해도 선호도가 낮아질 것을 고려하면 주가 상승은 제한적일 것으로 판단하기 
때문이다.

동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일 
해당 사항 없음

## Document 272: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사HD현대중공업(329180) 피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 - 기업.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

(329180) 
HD 현대중공업 
2026.10.29

AI로보틱스/방산/조선/운송 양승윤 
6139 / syyang0901@eugenefn.com

피크아웃 우려를 이겨낼 조선사 
 투자의견 BUY(유지) 
 목표주가 610,000원(하향) 
 현재주가 285,500원(10/28)

 3Q26 매출액은 5조 7,536억원(+30.2%yoy), 영업이익은 8,992억원
(+61.3%yoy, OPM 15.6%)을 기록. 영업이익 컨센서스 9,792억원 하회.  
 평균 환율이 약 79원 하락하며 영업이익 약 450억원이 줄었고, 조업
일수는 47일로 통상 계절성 대비 4일 추가 감소해 약 500억원의 이
익 감소 효과 발생. 임단협 추가 비용의 약 75%도 3분기에 선반영
(잔여분은 4분기 반영). 일회성 요인을 제외하면  이익 체력은 견조. 
 동사의 조선 부문 매출액은 4조 3,988억원(+44.0%yoy), 영업이익은 
6,463억원(+62.8%yoy, OPM 14.7%)을 기록. 상선 OPM은 14.7%로 
2Q26(17.8%) 대비 하락했으나 대부분 외생변수 영향. 3Q26 대형선 매출 
중 2023년 수주분 비중은 45%, 2024~2025년 수주분은 51%. 고선가 
물량의 매출 비중이 높아지는 2027년까지 수익성 개선 여력은 충분.  
 9월 말 누적 상선 수주는 138.0억달러로 연간 목표의 120%, 엔진기계는 
41.4억달러로 155%를 달성했으나 함정(13%)과 해양(5%)은 부진. LNG선
은 중국 조선소의 저가 수주와 발주 둔화로 선가가 일부 하락. 선주들이 현 
선가를 저점으로 인식하며 발주 협의를 재개하는 분위기. 북미 LNG 프로젝
트 FID가 이어지고 있어 2027년 LNG선 발주 회복 기대. 
 함정 부문은 매출 2,570억원(-14.6%qoq)으로 줄었으나, 수출 비중 확대에 
따른 믹스 개선으로 영업이익 386억원(+114%qoq, OPM 15.0%)을 기록. 
해양플랜트는 매출액 3,799억원(+35.3%yoy), 영업이익 1,265억원(OPM 
33.3%). C/O 180억원을 제외해도 Ruya 공정 본격화와 Trion 고수익 물량
으로 높은 수익성 유지. 2027년 일부 프로젝트 인도로 매출 감소가 예상되
나, LNG선·블록 물량 투입으로 고정비 부담을 완화할 계획. 신규 해상변전
소(OSS) 계약을 앞두고 있으며, 2027년 초 추가 수주 발표도 기대. 
 주목할 부분은 엔진기계. 영업이익 2,299억원(OPM 24.2%)으로 높은 D/F 
엔진 비중을 바탕으로 견조한 수익성 유지. 육상발전용 힘센엔진은 올해   
3GW를 확보하며 2029년까지 계획 캐파 수준의 물량을 이미 채움. 생산 
예상 물량은 2028년 약 1.5GW, 2029년 약 3GW이며, 신규 공장 구축 이
후 연간 생산능력은 약 4GW로 확대 예정. 북미 Hyperscaler와의 직접 협
상이 늘고 있으며, 힘센엔진을 활용한 부유식 발전설비(BMPP)로 FDC 시장
까지 대응 가능. AI 데이터센터 전력 수요가 2028년 이후 새로운 이익 축. 
 미국에서는 Kiewit과 합작법인 설립 계약 체결. 군수지원함 등 지원함 사업
부터 추진하며, 현지 조선소 건설 과정에서 HII에 선박 블록을 공급하는 방
안도 협의 중. 구체적인 투자계획은 2026년 말까지 확정 목표. 국내에서는 
핵추진잠수함 초기 예산이 2027년 반영되며 동사도 사업 참여를 준비 중. 
 동사에 대한 투자의견 BUY, 목표주가 61만원을 제시. 고선가 물량 매출 확
대와 육상발전용 엔진 성장으로 피크아웃을 이겨낼 굴지의 조선사.

당사는 동 자료를 기관투자가 또는 제 3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다 
조사분석담당자는 자료작성일 현재 동 종목과 관련하여 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 조사분석담당자 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다 
동 자료는 당사의 제작물로서 모든 저작권은 당사에게 있습니다 
동 자료는 당사의 동의 없이 어떠한 경우에도 어떠한 형태로든 복제, 배포, 전송, 변형, 대여할 수 없습니다 
동 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료 및 정보로부터 얻어진 것이나, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 어떠
한 경우에도 자료는 고객의 주식투자의 결과에 대한 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙자료로 사용될 수 없습니다

## Document 308: BTIG警告：美股已进入“陌生地带”，黄金或进一步下探至3500美元

Source PDF: all report pdf/BTIG警告：美股已进入“陌生地带”，黄金或进一步下探至3500美元.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 3
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

美股低波动实为板块极端分化制造的假象，内部结构极度脆弱。 BTIG 警告，市场亟需⼀场
恐慌性重挫洗盘，纳指⾯临⾄少 8.5% 的回撤，标普 500 下看 7300 点。此外，半导体已技术
破位， AI 板块或受⼤选政策反噬，⾦银均已筑顶，真正的底部远未到来。
摘要：
美股指数表⾯平静之下，内部正经历 2000 年以来罕⻅的剧烈撕裂。 BTIG Research 在 11 ⽉ 1 ⽇发
布的报告中警告，当前这场剧烈的板块轮动尚未结束，市场远未触及真正意义上的底部。
在过去五个交易⽇⾥，五个板块涨幅超过 2% ，同时两个板块跌幅超过 5% ，但 VIX 始终未能收于
20 上⽅ —— 这⼀看似⽭盾的组合，恰恰揭⽰了问题所在：波动率被横截⾯轮动所吸收，指数层
⾯的平静掩盖了内部结构的脆弱。 BTIG 认为，这种低 VIX 状态并不构成安全垫，相关性在下⾏
⽅向趋于⼀致的那⼀天尚未到来。
在关键位点上，标普 500 的⻛险下⾏⽬标指向 200 ⽇均线附近约 7300 点，费城半导体指数
（ SOXX ）的⻓期下⾏⽬标则落在 365 ⾄ 400 美元区间。与此同时，⻩⾦与⽩银已完成筑顶形

态， BTIG 对⻩⾦的下⾏⽬标为每盎司 3500 美元，⽩银为每盎司 40 美元。
表⾯波动率受抑，内部结构已然脆弱
报告称， VIX 未能收于 20 上⽅，在许多投资者眼中意味着市场尚在可控范围之内。但 BTIG 的分
析指向截然相反的结论。
五个板块单周涨幅超过 2% 、两个板块跌幅超过 5% ，这种极端的横截⾯分化意味着市场的真实波
动正在板块之间相互对冲，从⽽压低了指数层⾯的波动率读数。换⾔之， VIX 的低位并⾮市场稳
定的信号，⽽是轮动激烈程度的副产品 ——波动被横截⾯吸收，指数层⾯反⽽更加脆弱。
半导体板块的技术形态进⼀步印证了这⼀判断。等权重半导体 ETF （ XSD ）已第⼆次跌破 200 ⽇
均线，并逼近 7 ⽉低点的第三次测试。 BTIG 明确指出， “ 这不是底部应有的样⼦ ” 。费城半导体指
数（ SOXX ）同步破位， BTIG 认为其⻓期下⾏⽬标回归⾄ 4 ⽉突破点，即365 ⾄ 400 美元区间。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

历史形态显⽰：情绪远未洗清，下跌或未结束
BTIG 援引多项历史数据，论证当前市场情绪与技术结构均不⽀持 " 已经⻅底 " 的判断。
纳斯达克 100 指数出现了⾃ 1985 年以来第⼋次在历史⾼位形成负外包⽉形态。从历史经验看，前
七次发⽣后六个⽉均录得正回报，这对多头⽽⾔是⼀个安慰。但另⼀⾯同样不容忽视：前七次
⽆⼀例外地在随后六个⽉内出现⾄少 8.5% 的回撤，其中 1990 年和 2020 年的两次回撤幅度更超过
20% 。
情绪指标同样显⽰洗盘远未完成。纳斯达克每⽇情绪指数（ DSI ）⽬前收于 44 ，为 7 ⽉以来最
低，但7 ⽉的低点是 30 ， 3 ⽉的低点是 12—— 当前 44 的读数距离真正的恐慌性低点仍有相当距
离。
更关键的是，近⼀年内纽约证券交易所从未出现过单⽇下跌成交量占⽐超过 80% 的交易⽇，
BTIG 将这⼀现象描述为 " 史⽆前例 " 。在其看来，在出现⾄少⼀次、甚⾄数次这样的 " 相关性趋
⼀ " 式重挫之前，市场尚不具备重新进攻的条件。
信⽤市场也在发出警⽰。⾼收益债平均利差已升⾄ 340 个基点，创年内新⾼。此前市场担忧主要

集中于 CCC 级债券，但如今平均利差的⾛阔表明压⼒已向更⼴泛的信⽤市场蔓延。
关键位点：标普 500 看向 7300 ，防守端⻛险回报已变
在具体的⻛险位点上， BTIG 将标普 500 的下⾏⽬标锁定在 200 ⽇均线附近约 7300 点，即夏季低点
⼀带。这⼀位置将是多空双⽅的关键博弈区域。
防守性板块的⻛险回报格局也在悄然转变。必需消费品板块刚刚录得2000 年 10 ⽉以来最强单⽉
表现，⽉涨幅超过 11%，资⾦避险属性的集中释放令该板块短期性价⽐下降。公⽤事业板块同样
如此 ——BTIG 在 9 ⽉极度超卖时看好该板块，但在经历 11% 的反弹并触及关键阻⼒位后，其⻛险
回报已完全逆转，不再具备此前的吸引⼒。
贵⾦属⽅⾯， BTIG 认为⻩⾦与⽩银均已完成筑顶形态，维持谨慎⽴场，下⾏⽬标分别为⻩⾦每
盎司 3500 美元、⽩银每盎司 40 美元。
中期选举变量：堪萨斯翻蓝， AI 板块⾯临政策逆⻛
政治层⾯的不确定性为市场增添了新的变量。 BTIG 指出，⺠调与博彩市场的预测常常出错，但

当前数据显⽰⺠主党赢得堪萨斯州参议院席位的概率已超过 50%—— 堪萨斯州上⼀次在参议院

## Document 374: Indonesia_ Policy mix aligns (2026-10-12)

### Page 1

SHIFT IN NARRATIVE

After a rough first half of the year, the 
narrative around Indonesia’s economy has 
turned relatively constructive . The macro 
outlook is on the mend, and policy coherence 
has strengthened. Recent budget 
developments have reduced the likelihood of an 
outright fiscal slippage. A more disciplined 
fiscal trajectory should also provide room for 
monetary policy to stay focused on its price 
stability mandate rather than on ad dressing 
financial stability risks due to fiscal concerns.

On the markets front, e ven as external triggers 
impacted the rupiah, the one -sided sharp 
depreciation run in the currency in the first half 
of the year has stalled . Debt markets have  
attracted offshore interest, alongside strong 
demand for shorter-tenor SRBI securities, all of 
which have contributed to the overall capital 
flows picture.

Ahead of MSCI’s final take on the domestic 
equity market’s classification in November, 
however, equity markets  find little favour . 
Nonetheless, material improvement s in the 
stock listing s and regulatory requirements 
undertaken by the stock market and financial 
regulators are expected to contribute to the 
medium-term stability of the capital markets.

Key domestic risks that plagued the economy 
in the first half of the year have been alleviated 
to some extent, while a few other monitorables 
remain.

www.dbs.com/insightsdirect/macro groupresc@dbs.com 
• Indonesia's macro narrative has 
turned more constructive in 
2H26. 
• Key domestic policy concerns 
have eased. 
• Policy alignment has improved. 
• Greater fiscal discipline should 
allow BI to remain focused on 
price stability.  
• The current account is likely to 
widen as the trade surplus 
narrows. 
• Portfolio and FDI inflows should, 
however, narrow the BOP 
shortfall. 
• Few monitorables remain, 
including factors in the 2027 
fiscal math. 
• IDR performance will remain 
heavily influenced by global 
conditions.

Radhika Rao, Senior Economist 
radhikarao@dbs.com

### Page 2

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            2

NEAR-TERM FISCAL WORRIES 
RECEDE

Fiscal fears have moderated in the near 
term. New Finance Minister Suahasil 
Nazara reaffirmed the government’s 
commitment to maintain the annual 
deficit below -3% of GDP threshold. The 
2026 run-rate suggests that the deficit is 
likely to close within the budgeted target.

By September 2026, the fiscal deficit 
stood at 1.2% of GDP , reflecting a 
normalisation in spending and firm 
revenue growth. Total spending rose by 
a still -strong 1 9% yoy , even though the 
pace moderated from 1H, backed by 
disbursements towards the flagship free 
food program, subsidy and 
compensation outlays , as well as 
personnel expenditure.  Realised 
expenditure stood at 69% of the full -
year budget by 9M26.

On the other hand, revenues rose sharply 
by 25 .5% yoy (partly benefiting from 
base effects as readings were negative 
at the same time last year), helped by 
higher tax collections, export duties, and 
non-tax receipts. Revenues have already 
accounted for 74% of the budgeted 
scale by 9M26, suggesting full -year 
target is likely to be met.

Global crude prices are a key risk for the 
fiscal outlook, as prevailing Brent prices 
are above $100bl at the time of writing 
(vs budgeted ICP at $70bl), which will 
feed through higher subsidy and 
compensation outgoes, as authorities 
remain hesitant to raise fuel pump prices.

Significant efficiency measures have, 
however, been put in place  to contain 
spending, including reductions in 
outlays towards flagship schemes , 
which will help contain the extent of 
deterioration in the fiscal math. For 
instance, the flagship MBG program, 
which started with an allocation of IDR 
35trn, was  moderated to IDR 268trn 
(realised spending by Sep26 was IDR 
153trn). Successive stimulus packages

### Page 3

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            3

have been announced but the scale has 
been contained.

2027 math signals fiscal prudence, soft 
spots warrant attention

The official goalposts for 2027 point to 
a continued preference for fiscal 
prudence rather than fiscal activism.

Spending is on course to surpass the 
symbolic IDR 4000tn level, ris ing nearly 
4% yoy, with outlays divided between 
the centre and regional governments. 
The mix points to steady allocations 
towards subsidies, social transfers , and 
capital expenditure,  with ministry -level 
spending (K/L) set to contract, while non-
K/L spending is budgeted to increase. 
More notably, spending is expected to 
rise on key flagship programs – for 
instance, social protection (up 10%), the 
largest component education (up 14%) , 
which includes the free meals program 
at IDR 240trn.

On the revenue end, state revenues are 
expected to rise by 9% yoy compared to 
the budgeted 2026 APBN/ 7% vs the 
revised 2026 outlook. Compared to 
baseline 2026, tax revenues are 
expected to rise 10.5%, besides higher 
non-tax collections. This math lands the 
2027 budgeted deficit at -2.4% of GDP 
vs revised 2026 at -2.85% of GDP.

Few uncertainties that loom.  Firstly, 
next year’s math rests on an upbeat 
growth projection of 6 .0% (DBSf 5.2%) , 
besides modest depreciation in the 
rupiah and about  a 7% increase in the 
Indonesia crude price assumption (see 
table). Some of these expectations 
might be challenged as the year unfolds. 
Next, state revenues are expected to 
rise to a n upbeat  12.5% of GDP , likely 
riding on a strong growth forecast, 
better tax due diligence , and improved 
administration via the Coretax 
mechanism.

Thirdly, crude prices are a key risk to the 
fiscal math. Given the economy’s 
position as a net oil importer, various 
official commentaries put the sensitivity  
at every $1pb oil worsening the fiscal 
balance by IDR 6trn, after accounting for 
oil-related revenues. This implies that for 
every $10pb increase, the deficit could 
be impacted by ~IDR 60trn, not far from 
MOF’s sensitivity of IDR 68trn (~0.25 -
0.30% of GDP) due to higher subsidy and 
compensation costs  (ICT assumed at 
$75bl vs prevailing Brent at $100bl). 
Spending will need to reprioritised to 
accommodate additional allocation 
towards subsidies and compensation.

### Page 4

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            4

Next, the direction of travel on the 
contribution of SOE dividends from the 
investment agency to the state coffers 
for 2027 remains unclear, after being 
accounted for this year. Lastly, 
successive stimulus measures in-kind 
and wage subsidies were announced this 
year and could marginally add to the 
strain in 2027 (0.1-0.2% of GDP).

Discussions around the State Finance 
Law also warrant a watch. We maintain 
our deficit forecast at a wider -2.8% to -
2.9% range to account for any revenue 
shortfall or a gradual pace of growth. 
Nonetheless, deficits/debt are likely to 
stay below key thresholds  and not 
emerge as a flashpoint for the markets.

MONETARY POLICY ON AN EVEN 
KEEL

Bank Indonesia has left the benchmark 
rate unchanged  since July 2026, after 
100bp hikes in 1H26. While tighter global 
financial conditions and geopolitical 
risks were key risks on the horizon, the BI 
held the domestic GDP forecast at 4.9 -
5.7% (DBSf: 5.3%) alongside 
manageable inflation, notwithstanding 
El Nino-related risks. Inflation rose to 3.3% 
yoy in Sep26, towards the high end of the 
target range. Authorities have, however, 
assured that the impact of higher food 
prices due to poor weather will be 
addressed through administrative 
measures by regional bodies.

Contained inflation expectations ha ve 
reduced the urgency for BI to tighten 
policy further, allowing policymakers to 
prioritise growth and financial stability 
considerations. The emphasis on 
strengthening the balance of payments 
position reflects continued caution over 
external financing conditions and capital 
flows. However, with the rupiah 
expected to remain stable on the back of 
policy measures and fundamenta ls, the 
BI did not view current external 
pressures as severe enough to warrant 
additional tightening.

BI introduced a host of measures at the 
June to September policy meetings , 
including FX monetary hedging 
instruments and a mechanism to provide 
foreign investors with greater flexibility 
in managing IDR-denominated 
investments in Indonesia.  
These included –  
a) swap hedging per tenor bucket was 
increased to 3M - 15%; 6M - 20%; 12M - 
25%, from 12.5% earlier.  
b) DNDF incentive was increased to 6M - 
25% and 12M – 30%. An increase in FX/ 
NDF swap premium incentives for longer 
tenors will lower hedging costs and

### Page 5

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            5

encourage longer tenor positions, in turn 
increasing longevity of these flows;  
c) Launch of the VASTRA scheme (FX 
Market Transactions for Hedging 
through Partner Banks) got underway, 
with the naming of 14 domestic partner 
banks. This facility is designed to help 
investors transact with lower underlying 
documentation requirements, tap into 
longer banking hours , and access 
onshore hedging facilities.  
d) deepen FX and money markets by 
expanding Local Currency Transactions 
(LCT) between IDR and CNY , besides a 
few other currencies.

Policymakers are likely to tap a 
combination of administrative and 
macroprudential measures rather than 
solely interest rates to defend the 
currency as well as keep inflationary 
expectations in check.

We expect the pause on rates to extend 
into 4Q26.  This call might be at risk if 
depreciation pressures on the rupiah 
mount again on unfavourable global 
developments and if the US Fed 
undertakes a more aggressive hik ing 
cycle than our baseline (of 75bp hikes).

WIDER CAD WILL BE PARTLY 
OFFSET BY POSITIVE FLOWS

Indonesia’s 1H26 current account 
deficit (CAD) widened significantly  to -
$16bn vs -$3bn in the comparable period 
a year ago. Much of this deterioration 
was led by a narrower goods trade 
balance, while  the services deficit 
widened on the seasonal travel account 
due to the Hajj period. Higher coupon 
payments weighed on the primary 
account.  This pressure is likely to persist 
in 2H as the oil & gas trade deficit 
remains wide and export performance 
has been fi rm but tracking lower than 
regional peers, likely also impacted by 
the El Nino phenomenon in key agri 
commodities. We expect full year CAD to 
be closer to 1.8-2% of GDP from -0.1% in 
2025.

Much rests on the capital flows account. 
While 1H balance of payments (BOP) also 
widened, there was palpable 
improvement in 2Q26 vs 1Q.  2Q BOP 
narrowed significantly to $0.9bn from a 
wide $9.1bn in 1Q, helped by positive 
capital account flows – primarily 
stronger portfolio investment on SRBI 
and debt inflows, and net FDI position. 
This dependency is likely to extend to 2H, 
with capital fl ows till Sep26 holding up 
on account of hedging discounts and 
moves to improve inflows. Global 
conditions, including the narrow ID -US 
yields, however, warrant attention. 
Nonetheless, full-year BOP could remain

### Page 6

Indonesia: Policy mix aligns October 12, 2026 
                                                                                                                                            6

wide, on a scale comparable to the 2025 
gap.

IN SUMMARY

IDR markets have been relatively 
steady in October despite higher oil 
prices and a bid dollar , though 
sentiment remains sensitive to external 
developments and swings in global risk 
appetite. USDIDR traded within 17600 -
18000, while 10Y yield hovered around 
7.15-7.25%. A jump in the Aug goods 
trade surplus aided this respite, 
alongside foreign purchases of domestic 
debt, which have remained positive this 
month and on a  YTD basis. Insurers, the 
central bank, and retail investors have 
remained key anchors for the bond 
market. SRBI rates are off highs to sub -
7% levels but are attracting steady 
demand, likely encouraged by hedging 
discounts. An improved outlook for flows 
has helped contain spillovers from a 
wider current account deficit. This does 
not take away from the fact that the 
rupiah is amongst the worst performers 
on a year-to-date basis.

Separately, markets will also monitor 
developments in the political economy, 
institutional architecture, and formation, 
as well as the role of state -led agencies 
(e.g., DSI) and progress on the 
centralisation of decision -making. We 
expect the BI to keep on hold, however, 
pressure to tighten policy might return if 
the US Fed hikes rates well into 2027 
and the US dollar benefits from a risk-off 
environment, weighing on the currency.

► 美國8月CPI月增0.4%、年增3.4%符合 市場
預期，核心CPI月增0.3%，年增率2.4%高於
預期的2.3%，通膨未降使聯 準會升息一碼 ，
美國10年期公債殖利率彈升，創2007年來新
高 。 繼上週歐洲 央 行 將 存 款 利 率 調 升 至
2.50%後，日本銀行 也加速升 息，基準利率
來到 1.25% 、 態 度 轉 鷹， 全球債 市先跌後
漲，因部分投資人預期債 市已提前反映升息
風險，高殖利率開始吸引資金進場鎖利。
► 聯準會緊縮後年底前仍有升息一碼預期 ，美
元指數重回百元之上 ，非美貨幣 貶值。加密
貨幣監管的重要法案《 CLARITY法案》，在
美國參議院終止辯論程序 性表決中 ，法案以
49票對50票未獲通過 ，無法進入最終表決程
序，虛擬資產聞訊下 挫。沙烏地阿拉伯關閉
東西輸油管線，佔全球原油供應 量約4-5%，
加深能源供需失衡 疑慮，沙國透過阿曼港口

出口、緩解供給 ，油價先漲後跌 ，西德州油
價保持每桶百元高價 。聯準會會後 ，美債利
率利空出盡下滑，激勵金、銅價反彈。

► 聯準會9月升息1碼至3.75%-4.00%，最新點
陣圖顯示2026年仍有再升息一碼空間 ，2027
年傾向維持利率不變 ，2028年後才逐步回到
降息趨勢 。主席華許重申抑制通膨目標 ，升
息立場官員數明顯較前次會議增加 ，凸顯聯
準會鷹派政策態度 ，惟利率路徑較市場原先
預期的連續緊縮和緩，市場偏利多解讀。
► 聯準會 本次亦上修 2026-2027年GDP成長預
估，下修失業率預測 ，反映美國 經濟與勞動
市場尚未明顯轉弱；另一方面 ，能源價格上
升與成本轉嫁使整體及核心通膨預估再度上

修；景氣具韌性 、通膨回落速度偏慢 ，使聯
準會仍有條件維持較緊縮的政策立場 。在高
利率環 境可 能延 續 下，股 票評價 仍面 臨壓
力，配置上可相對偏向獲利與現金流穩定 、
防禦性較高的產業；債券則以中短天期 、高
信評企業債為主要方向 ，把握目前較高的收
益率。長天期公債仍受通膨 、財政紀律及期
限溢酬上升影響 ，宜待通膨與景氣出現更明
確降溫訊號後，再提高配置。

► 日本8月CPI年增1.9%，油價上漲帶來的外溢
影響，逐漸反應在消費成本上。7月勞工平均
薪資較去年同期成長 4.7%，創1997年1月以
來最大增幅；薪資增速追上通膨 ，帶動實質
薪資增長 。此外 ，第二季 GDP季增年率達
1.4%優於預期，8月製造業PMI因AI及半導體
需求暢旺上升，7月出口年增率高達23.2%，
創2022年10月來最快增速 。整體通膨升溫加
上景氣復甦，為日銀升息提供底氣。
► 日本央行會後 聲明稿表示：金融環境實質利
率偏低 ，將視情況調整貨幣政策 ，並強調升
息後金融環境仍維持寬鬆 ，未如市場預期的
鷹派，使日股持續上漲 、日圓回貶 。考量日
銀呵護 寬鬆金融環境 ，侷限日圓續強力 道，
但在美 國施 壓與 日 銀未來 仍可能 升息 背景
下，日圓難回到過去極弱 狀態。相較過去 ，
現在升值的日圓對日本出口 、科技股壓力可
控，日本10年公債殖利率來到3%，利率上升
有利 金融 股 ，投 資 短期或 以金融 、內 需為

主，科技、半導體股逢回可分批布局。
經濟與通膨升溫，日銀加快緊縮步調

### Page 7

投資焦點低波價值防禦
► 美、歐、日主要央行連袂升息 ，加上美伊衝
突惡化 ，國際油價高漲 ，通膨疑慮與成熟國
家債務壓力 ，使全球多數國家公債殖利率衝
高，市場擔憂高利率與高油價可能拖累經濟
成長，全球股債匯市承壓 、波動上升 ，並以
此前領漲多頭行情的晶片 、半導體和科技股
最明顯 。觀察今年以來 ，不止股市波動度上
升，債市的波動更不亞於 股票市場 ，統計過
去，當VIX恐慌指數與MOVE債券波動度走高
時，低波動或防禦股的表現 ，往往比成長股
更好，顯示當市場震盪加劇時 ，資金傾向轉
往穩健資產停泊。
► 最近一次聯準會升息 ，為2022 年因疫情爆
發、供應鏈中斷 ，導致通膨大幅上揚 ，迫使
央行採取緊縮貨幣政策 ，和目前通膨上升促
央行升息背景 類似。前次聯準會啟動升息後
數月，成長股 、費城半導體股表現受抑 ，防
禦股、價值股和低波動族群 ，在剛開始升息
的環境下 ，表現相對抗跌 。考量目前聯準 會
籠罩在升息陰霾下，可增加相關類股配置。
對等關稅宣布後，內需防禦股獲利下修幅度最少 最近一次聯準會升息後，防禦低波高息和價值股突出美國股債波動度上升時，低波動、防禦股表現較佳

金融市場波動上升，增配低波、防禦價值股更穩健

### Page 8

8
► 觀察最近一季 ，資訊科技 、通訊服務與非核
心消費等High Beta類股，表現明顯落後於大
盤；反觀價值股 、防禦股和低波動等因子 ，
甚至核 心消 費與 高 股利等 傳統類 防禦 性產
業，報酬相對較 穩健，受資金青睞 。顯示在
經歷過 上半 年科 技 、半導 體股的 大漲 行情
後，市場預期高 利率與地緣政治 風險可能維
持更久時間 ，重新聚焦在獲利持久、現金易
落袋為安的防禦型資產，更能持盈保泰。
► 從預估本 益比看，目前金融僅 16倍、防禦股
17.5倍、價值股 (以IWD ETF代表)17.9倍、
低波動 (以USMV ETF代表)19.6倍，皆低於
標普500指數 現在 的21.3倍，近月資金流向
物美價廉的低波動價值 、防禦族群，評價越
低、漲幅越高 。展望後市，AI長線基本面仍
持續看好 ，股市回檔或為評價 修正而 非盈餘
下滑，市場波動度提升之際 ，建議不過度 集
中於單一 產業或 個股，多元配置適度增加 低
波動 、 防禦 和價 值股，分 批布局 以控管 風

險，料將更能在震盪加劇時安穩求利。
投資焦點

資金輪動，關注波動度低和評價合理的防禦價值族群

### Page 9

9
股市
 美、歐、日央行升息抑通膨 ，利率創高添股市波動風險 ，加上成熟國家債務攀升 ，科技巨擘亦舉債
投入資本支出 ，未來獲利與變現 能力持續被市場檢視 ，金融市場震盪不減 。美伊衝突升溫，近期可
透過防禦、低波動或價值 股來降低風險；科技 及AI半導體等發展趨勢不變 ，獲利基本面佳 ，長線發
展可期，宜採逢回分批布局方式，分散國家與產業配置。
 歐、日股可作為分散配置重點 ，全球利率彈升有利銀行股 ，歐、日銀行股基本面佳 ，歐洲國防軍工
仍具投資吸引力。
策略風格：短期可增加防禦股配置，中長
期看好AI題材如科技及半導體，非AI部分
可關注金融等產業
區域：日本銀行股、歐洲金融、國防
債市
 全球公債殖利率攀升，應避免超長天期債券投資 ，預期年底前美國 10年期公債利率將於 4.5%-5.0%
震盪，可逢利率走升時，以中短天期債券進行鎖利領息 。高信評債體質較佳 ，具穩定現金流與還款
能力，聚焦風險調整後利差具吸引力者，包含金融、科技、通訊及公用事業等產業。
 可以非美貨幣債券如歐元或澳幣計價投資級債券，分散單一貨幣風險。

債種：中天期高信評債鎖利，產業如金融、
科技、通訊及公用事業等
衛星配置：非美貨幣債券分散風險
匯市
 聯準會升息且態度偏鷹，美元高檔盤整。
 日本銀行9月升息後，年底前或明年初仍可能再度緊縮，日圓震盪加大偏升。
美元高檔整理，日圓震盪加大偏升
商品
 沙烏地阿拉伯關閉輸油管線 ，加上荷姆茲海峽局勢緊張 ，能源供需失衡 ，油價年底前維持 偏高水位。
 短期受美國雙率走高有壓 ，惟美日財政赤字壓力 與去美元化使全球央行買盤依舊 ，提供黃金中長期
穩定支撐。
短期黃金有壓，中長期維持偏多看法
類別 市場看法 偏好資產

### Page 16

個別客戶因年齡、投資能力、金融商品交易經驗及風險承受度不同，適合之服務及/或金融商品亦有不同，應進一步與專屬理財業務人員進行評估。投資人應徹底瞭解相關服務及金融商品之風險 ，並審慎評估個人財力、資產狀況、
理財需求及風險承受能力，獨立判斷並自行決定是否投資及承擔有關風險。投資任何商品皆具有風險，商品以往之績效不保證未來之投資收益，本公司不負責商品之盈虧，也不保證最低之收益。
本公司保留對本文件及其內容之著作權及其他相關權利，任何人未經本公司同意，不得翻印或作其他任何用途使用(包括但不限於傳送或轉發予其他人士等)。如有違反情事，本公司除不負任何責任外，亦將對未經同意之使用與散布
等行為究責。
基金警語
投資一定有風險，基金投資有賺有賠，申購前應詳閱公開說明書
【信託產品投資須知及風險預告：（請投資人審慎評估下列各項條文內容）】
1. 基金（包括具有定期配息之基金）投資具投資風險，此一風險可能使信託本金發生全部虧損。
2. 基金委託人須自負盈虧，本公司受託投資不保本不保息，除盡善良管理人注意義務外，投資所可能產生的本金虧損、匯率損失、或基金解散、清算、移轉、合併等風險，均由委託人承擔。

3. 本公司受託投資之基金經金管會核准或同意生效，惟不表示絕無風險。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，委託人
申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 投資前應詳閱風險預告及各該基金公開說明書 ，確實作好財務規劃與風險評估 。本公司受託投資之基金已備有公開說明書 (或其中譯本)或投資人須知【前述文件併揭露有關基金應負擔之費用 (境外基金含分銷費用)】，委託人可
至本公司營業處所索取或至公開資訊觀測站、基金資訊觀測站或本公司網站下載。
5. 為保護既有投資人之權益，部份境外基金設有價格稀釋調整機制及公平市價規定，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書及投資人須知。
6. 委託人瞭解並同意受託人辦理特定金錢信託業務(即委託人委託投資基金)交易時，自交易相對人取得之報酬、費用、折讓等各項利益，得作為受託人收取之信託報酬；另委託人在持有基金期間 ，受託人仍持續收受經理費分成報
酬，委託人可至受託人(即本公司)網站 (https://www.kgi.com.tw/zh-tw/)查詢基金之通路報酬。其基金經理費率及其分成費率與其他通路報酬最新變動情形，亦可至該網站查詢。

7. 後收型基金在贖回時，基金公司將依持有期間長短收取不同比率之遞延申購手續費 ，該費用將自贖回總額中扣除；另後收型基金若有收取分銷費 ，則申購手續費雖可遞延收取，惟每年仍需支付0%~1.06%的分銷費（按基金日平
均淨資產價值計算），可能造成實際負擔費用增加（分銷費依各基金公司而有不同，請參考本公司後收型基金投資費用暨特約事項及各基金之投資人須知），另該費用已反映於每日基金淨值中，委託人無需額外支付。
8. 委託人之交易如經基金公司認定為短線交易者，基金公司或本公司可拒絕受理申請轉換或申購要求，並可請求支付一定比例之贖回費用或其他相關費用，費用標準均依各該基金公司之規定。
9. 委託人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績效亦不代表未來績效之保證。基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動，投資人於獲配息時，宜一併注意基
金淨值之變動。又投資新興市場可能比投資已開發國家有較大的價格波動及流動性較低的風險，委託人應慎選投資標的。
10. 部分基金可能持有衍生性商品部位，該部位可能超過基金淨資產價值之100%，並可能因而造成基金淨值高度波動及衍生其他風險。

11. 基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部分，可能導致原始投資金額減損；且部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之費用。由本金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至證券投資信託事業或總代理人之公司
網站查詢。
12. 非投資等級債券基金（本類基金主要係投資於非投資等級之高風險債券且配息來源可能為本金）（主要特色係投資前揭基金之組合基金亦同），適合能承受較高風險之『非保守型』委託人且不宜占投資組合過高的比重。由於非
投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損，不適合無法承擔相關風險之委託人；又如
係本國投信機構募集者，則可能投資於符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，雖其投資總金額不得超過基金淨資產價值之百分之三十，然該債券具有債券發行人違約之信用風險、利率風險、流動性風險、交易對手風險
及因財務訊息揭露不完整或因價格不透明導致波動性較大之風險，投資人應審慎評估；另證券投資信託事業募集之債券型基金、平衡型基金、不動產證券化基金、以追蹤、模擬或複製標的指數表現之指數股票型基金（ETF）及

指數型基金或募集之基金投資新興市場國家債券達基金淨資產價值之百分之六十以上者，或有可能投資符合美國Rule 144A規定具有私募性質之債券，亦應留意相關風險。
13. 投資於中國之基金應遵守中華民國相關法令之規定：境外基金 (不含經金管會核准向證交所申請上市或交易之境外 ETF)如有投資大陸地區之有價證券者，以掛牌上市有價證券及銀行間債券市場為限 ，且投資前述有價證券總金額
不得超過該境外基金淨資產價值之百分之二十(境外基金機構依鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫向金管會申請並經認可者得適用鼓勵境外基金深耕計畫所列放寬前揭投資總金額上限至百分之四十之優惠措施 )；證券投資信託基金雖可投資
大陸地區之有價證券，惟仍應符合信託契約、公開說明書、證券投資信託基金管理辦法等相關規定及受到基金公司QFII額度之限制。又投資人須留意中國市場之政府政策、法令、會計稅務制度、經濟與市場等變動所可能產生之
投資風險。
14. 各基金於公開（含簡式）說明書或投資人須知揭露之風險報酬等級，係依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」而訂定，本公司亦依據投信投顧公會「基金風險報酬等級分類標準」訂定基金產品風險等級。

富管理客服專線或透過財富管理客服信箱服務，查詢申訴案件處理進度。

## Document 421: Riksbank to hike despite soft inflation and rallying krona

Source PDF: all report pdf/Riksbank to hike despite soft inflation and rallying krona.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

We expect a 25bp hike on 4 November by the Riksbank,
alongside a reiteration of the tightening bias. Inﬂation was
softer than expected in September, but upside risks remain for
prices, and a resilient economy allows policymakers to tighten
pre-emptively. We have revised our EUR/SEK year-end target to
11.00 after the recent rally
Francesco Pesole
We expect two rate hikes in Sweden in the next few months
The Riksbank announces policy on 4 November, and we are sticking

to our call for a 25bp rate hike to 2.0%. Market bets on a rate
increase next week have dwindled in the past week or so, moving
from 22-24bp to 15-18bp. While we don’t see a single clear trigger
for this partial dovish rethink, a soft inﬂation print in September
and SEK appreciation may have both played a role.
However, both in-meeting and oﬀ-meeting commentary by
Riksbank oﬃcials have been endorsing a move at this November

### Page 2

meeting, and the rise in energy prices oﬀsets the dovish inﬂation
and currency market inputs, in our view.
Don’t expect an explicit commitment to further increases, but the
tightening bias should remain in place. Our call remains that the
Riksbank will skip December and hike again to 2.25% on 3
February. We don’t currently expect any further rate increases
beyond that, but the risks are clearly on the hawkish side given the
persistence of energy price inﬂation.

Muted inﬂation in September, but goods
prices are picking up
Swedish inﬂation was slightly more subdued than expected in
September. CPIF came in at 1.5% year-on-year as expected, but
CPIF excluding energy was just 0.5%, 0.2ppt below the Riksbank’s
forecast. The softness was concentrated in food and services, with
an unusually large drop in car rental prices playing a role.
But the details were not entirely dovish. Goods inﬂation was
stronger and broad-based, with durable goods prices rising 1.1%

month-on-month and annual inﬂation turning positive for the ﬁrst
time in over a year. That ﬁts with earlier signals from producer
prices and suggests higher global price pressures are starting to
feed through.
We remain aligned with the Riksbank’s projections that CPIF
inﬂation should reach 2.5% and CPIF excluding energy 1.0% in
December. Further on, the outlook remains for further acceleration
in core measures, with CPIF excluding energy set to pass 2.0% by

the summer.
Growth proved resilient again in 3Q, with GDP rising 0.4% quarter-
on-quarter and 2.8% YoY.
Higher energy prices to tilt the balance
towards November
These inﬂation projections are only facing upside risks the longer
energy prices remain elevated. Alongside them, the Riksbank

signalled a split probability rates would be raised at either the

While the September inﬂation print and stronger krona (which we
address below as a topic) may argue for a pause, the rise in energy
prices is a bigger deciding factor in our view, and should tilt the
balance towards a rate hike already next week. In September, the
Brent crude price projection for end-2026 was $92/bbl, against the
current price of $118. European gas prices have also increased

c.35% since the September meeting. New projections are only
published in December, but we think the Riksbank will have to
acknowledge that higher energy prices increase the risk of more
persistent inﬂation.
On top of that, Riksbank oﬃcials have been generally hawkish-
leaning in their comments in the past month. Governor Erik
Thedeen said there are “strong reasons” to hike in the near term,
partly due to the resilient economy. Aino Bunge and Per Jansson

have both broadly endorsed that view in later comments.
Higher SEK, higher energy since last policy
meeting
Source: ING, Riksbank, Reﬁnitiv
Riksbank shouldn’t underestimate SEK
fragility if it holds

### Page 4

There’s no denying that the hawkish shift in September was partly
due to the Swedish krona’s weakness. Since that last policy
meeting, the krona has appreciated c.1.4% on a trade-weighted
basis, driven by a drop in EUR/SEK. That, in turn, has been a
consequence of a tightening in two-year swap rate diﬀerentials of
around 20bp, mirroring a stable outlook for Riksbank tightening
while ECB expectations were revised lower on the back of French-
related risk.

EUR/SEK was also trading 1-2% above our short-term fair value
estimates at the end of September. The subsequent rise in euro
idiosyncratic risk has pushed the pair into a modest 0.5-1%
undervaluation.
We don’t think that is enough to justify a dovish Riksbank surprise.
Despite the recent rebound, SEK remains 4% weaker on a trade-
weighted basis since the start of the year. More importantly, the
hawkish Riksbank stance is itself an endogenous driver of recent

SEK strength, with short-term rate diﬀerentials overwhelmingly
dominant for the currency.
A hold or dovish hike would trigger fresh SEK weakness. With the
energy story still negative and contingent euro idiosyncratic risk
potentially abating, SEK could return to late September levels again
without the Riksbank’s support.
Revising our EUR/SEK target lower
Our EUR/SEK year-end target has hovered around 11.10-11.15 for
the past couple of months. The build-up of substantial EUR-speciﬁc

risk makes a lower target an obvious adjustment. Markets are also
not fully pricing a Riksbank hike next week, with 17bp priced at the
time of writing, nor a February follow-up move, with 40bp priced,
which remains our baseline.
Still, EUR/SEK looks a bit too weak relative to fundamentals. That
points to a lingering risk premium and leaves room for upside if
some France-related risk abates into year-end (our baseline). At the
same time, the external backdrop remains challenging for SEK, with

oil prices still very elevated.

We are revising our year-end EUR/SEK target to 11.00, close to
current spot. Over the next week, however, we could see further

downside exploration, with a potential break below 10.90, as a
Riksbank hike may combine with some US-related risk around the
midterms to support SEK, both through the rates channel and
potential capital repatriation ﬂows.
In the longer run, we still expect a gradual decline to 10.70 over the
course of next year.

## Document 472: What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity continues to turn the screw_ (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity continues to turn the screw_ (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
WHAT LESSONS CAN WE LEARN TO KEEP EUROPEAN INDUSTRY COMPETITIVE FROM
THE MEASURES TAKEN BY GERMANY, AS THE COST OF ELECTRICITY CONTINUES TO
TURN THE SCREW?
Marianne Mueller and Pascal Devaux
issue 19
21 October 2026
The 2022 energy crisis clearly highlighted the issue of electricity prices in Europe — and, more broadly, the issue of energy prices. As
a result, European manufacturers’ competitiveness has been severely undermined when compared with the United States and China.

By reigniting tensions over electricity prices, the conflict that began in the Middle East in late February 2026 further underscores the
continent’s vulnerability to fossil fuel imports, despite the expansion of renewable energy. Faced with an environment of potential sharp
and sudden price rises, European governments do not have the same room for manoeuvre.
While the additional costs borne by European manufacturers are mainly linked to the share of gas in national electricity mixes, we will

first illustrate that, in Germany, grid operation and maintenance costs, as well as taxation, are also contributing significantly to these
costs.
We will then explore the solutions proposed by the German government under the new European measures regulating state aid to
electricity-intensive industries.
Finally, our estimates will show that, while these measures make Germany more competitive against some European countries, they
do not place it on an equal competitive footing with the United States or China. Nevertheless, Germany has made significant progress

in decarbonising and making its electricity mix more flexible from a European perspective. This should help to ensure that electricity costs
are reduced in the medium term.

### Page 2

2
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoInsight 19 / 21 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
worldWHAT LESSONS CAN WE LEARN TO KEEP EUROPEAN INDUSTRY COMPETITIVE FROM THE MEA-SURES TAKEN BY GERMANY, AS THE COST OF ELECTRICITY CONTINUES TO TURN THE SCREW?
GAS: A KEY FACTOR IN THE ADDITIONAL ELECTRICITY COSTS
FACED BY EUROPEAN INDUSTRY
European manufacturers are still paying significantly higher elec -
tricity costs than their American and Chinese competitors. In 2019,

there was a 30% difference between the prices charged to European
electricity-intensive industries (corresponding to the lower end of the
industrial price range) and the average US industrial price. In 2025,
it stood at nearly 100% according to the IEA (2026) 1, after peaking at
an average of 148% between 2022 and 2023. There is currently a 50%
difference with their Chinese competitors2.
The disruption to Russian gas supplies since 2022 is behind the rising

gas prices, particularly as the EU has had to turn to liquefied natural
gas3 (LNG), which is more expensive than gas transported via pipe -
line and, above all, exposes Europe to global gas market conditions.
The growth of LNG has, in fact, intensified competition between the
major consumption regions, primarily Europe and Asia. European and
Asian prices now tend to move more in tandem (Chart 1). However, in
China, the prevalence of coal — a widely available local resource —

and a more regulated electricity price help to keep industrial prices
lower. At the same time, the United States enjoys plentiful domestic
gas, which is produced at a low cost, driven by the sharp rise in shale
oil and gas production over the past decade. By contrast, since 2022,
Europe has been more exposed to the cost of imported gas (90% of its
total consumption, according to Eurostat), while the United States and
China have enjoyed plentiful domestic resources.

Fluctuations and the level of gas prices are passed on to Eu -
ropean electricity prices via the ‘merit order’ marginal pricing
mechanism, with the wholesale price determined by the margi -
nal cost of the last (and most expensive) electricity source called
upon to meet demand.
As a result, gas plays a decisive role, with gas-fired power sta -
tions setting the price in around 40% of cases in Europe4, with si-
gnificant variations depending on national electricity mixes. It is

close to this average in Germany, below it in Spain (26%) and
France (17%), and above it in Italy (73%) 5 in 2025 6 7. Therefore,
electricity prices are still sensitive to fluctuations in gas prices,
even when electricity is not generated from gas (Chart 2). This
pass-through is particularly pronounced when demand rises
and requires the use of more expensive sources, or when supply
constraints arise (for example, during extreme heatwaves that
reduce wind-power generation or disrupt the cooling of nuclear

power plants).
1 Prices – Electricity 2026 – Analysis – IEA
2 The difference may be even larger for other manufacturers who do not receive the same support as electricity-intensive sectors.
3 Imports of Russian gas (via pipeline and LNG) accounted for around 45% of total European imports in 2021, compared with 14% in Q2 2026 (Bruegel, natural gas imports).
4 ACER, 2026, Key developments in EU electricity and gas markets, 2026 Monitoring report.
5 In Italy, in response to sharply rising gas prices in 2026, the government is currently negotiating a potential option of capping the prices of gas supplied to power stations in

order to limit the rise in the wholesale price of electricity. However, this can only be a temporary general mechanism, with the potentially major cost being borne by public funds
and/or households.
6 Eurelectric, 2026, Power Barometer.
7 EMBER, 2026, Latest energy shock reminds Europe of its risky reliance on gas.
8 Energy shock: 2026 vs. 2022 dashboard – 2nd October 2026 – Economic Studies – BNP Paribas
However, for the time being, the 2026 energy crisis is having a far

smaller effect on electricity prices than in 2022. On the one hand, the
2026 peak reached by the European TTF benchmark is still well below
that recorded in 20228.
In addition, there has been significant progress in decarbonising Eu -
ropean electricity mixes since 2022, which has helped to mitigate, on
average, the pass-through of gas prices to electricity prices.

SOME EUROPEAN COUNTRIES HAVE MANAGED TO STAY
COMPETITIVE, BUT GERMANY IS STILL THE EXCEPTION
Electricity prices partly vary due to countries’ electricity mixes
(Chart 3). Electricity-intensive manufacturers in countries where the
share of fossil energy is high face higher costs (Poland, Italy and Ire -
land) (Chart 4). Conversely, countries with a less carbon-intensive mix

(particularly France and Spain in recent times) are less directly ex -
posed to these fluctuations, with costs closer to the United States and
China (Chart 5).
The situation in Germany is paradoxical, as, despite progressively
greening its electricity mix and wholesale electricity prices (averaging
90 EUR/MWh over the 2023–2025 period) and average electricity costs
(across all industrial sectors) in line with the European average, the most
energy-intensive industries face higher costs than the European average

(and even more so compared with international competitors). This is
mainly due to grid costs and a tax burden above EU standards (Chart 6).
9 Eurostat data are used to compare prices with the rest of the EU. However, given that electricity prices are not uniform for businesses at a European level, there may be differences
between Eurostat and national sources. Jäger P ., 2025, “Cautionary tale or model for Europe? How member states should react to Germany’s new electricity subsidy”, Hertie School,

Jacques Delors Centre.
10 P . Devaux, 2026, “European Union: low carbon transition and energy sovereignty, a path fraught with obstacles”
In the first half of 2026, for the most electricity-intensive industries,
the difference compared with the European average was 9%, based on
the prices excluding taxes and levies provided by Eurostat. This gap is
mainly due to the cost of accessing the grid. If all taxes and levies are
included, the difference rises to 21% (Chart 7)9.

EUROPE’S APPROACH OF ADOPTING BOTH STRUCTURAL
PROGRAMMES AND TARGETED SUPPORT MEASURES
European programmes to support the low-carbon transition pursue
objectives relating to climate action (reducing greenhouse gas emis -
sions), sovereignty10 (increasing energy self-sufficiency) and competi -
tiveness (reducing the volatility and costs associated with fossil fuels).
The RePowerEU programme, which was launched in 2022 in response
to the energy crisis, primarily aims to reduce imports of Russian gas

and, more generally, to accelerate the low-carbon transition. Although
Europe has reduced its dependence on imports of Russian fossil fuels,
there has still not been enough progress around the energy transition
and cross-border connectivity. This is due to governance constraints

Member State11.
State aid is still the main vehicle for this. In response to the 2021–
2022 energy crisis, the European Union temporarily relaxed the rules
on this type of aid in order to support businesses facing rising energy
prices. Any intervention of this kind, provided that it is selective and

could affect trade within the single market, must be authorised in ad -
vance by the European Commission in principle. Since June 2025, the
Clean Industrial Deal State Aid Framework (CISAF) has provided a more
sustainable framework for these kinds of interventions. In particular,
this framework, which runs until the end of 2030, authorises Member
States to grant temporary aid to electricity-intensive businesses to off-
set the cost of electricity. The aid must be targeted and come with

decarbonisation commitments12. Against the backdrop of the crisis in
the Middle East, the temporary METSAF scheme, adopted in April 2026,
helped (in relation to measures falling under the CISAF) to increase the
proportion of additional costs offset by governments from 50% to 70%,
thereby offering additional support to the most at-risk sectors.
GERMANY IS STILL SUFFERING FROM INSUFFICIENT IN-
VESTMENT IN ITS ELECTRICITY NETWORKS, WHICH THE
AID SCHEMES ARE ATTEMPTING TO OFFSET

The issue of electricity costs is a particularly hot issue in Germany,
given the importance of industry to the economy (around 20% of va -
lue added, one of the highest levels in Europe) and its exposure to
international competition. Furthermore, the delay in modernising
electricity infrastructure (only 22.6% of the high-voltage lines planned
for 2025 had actually been completed by that date) is causing bottle -
necks between the production areas in the north and the consump -

11 European Court of Auditors, 2026, “Implementation of the REPowerEU plan must be stepped up”.
12 It applies to the most electricity-intensive activities, which are seeing their competitiveness threatened by international competition, and follows the 4x50 principle: 50% of
electricity consumption is eligible for a subsidy; Of the eligible proportion, the subsidy may not exceed 50% of the wholesale price;
The final wholesale price paid by the company must not be less than €50/MWh;

50% of the aid received must be reinvested in decarbonisation (renewable energy generation, storage and improvements in energy efficiency); Around 90 sectors are covered by this
reform, accounting for approximately 2,000 companies, including in the chemical, steel and paper industries. The scheme, which is strictly regulated, will run for just three years
(from 1st January 2026 to the end of 2028) at an estimated total cost of between EUR 3 and 5 bn, financed by the special Climate and Transformation Fund.

13 Bundesnetzagentur Monitoring Report (2015) and Bundesnetzagentur Monitoring Report (2025). These costs mainly cover the costs of physical infrastructure; operational,
regulatory and compliance costs; and costs associated with using the transmission and distribution network.
14 The Industriestrompreis (industrial electricity price) is the public subsidy mechanism aiming to reduce the price of electricity for some industries.
15 The indirect carbon cost compensation mechanism is a government support measure for electricity-intensive industries, which aims to offset the cost of carbon-emission

allowances for energy producers.
tion centres in the south. As a result, the costs of accessing services
have increased fivefold over 10 years (rising from EUR 1 bn in 2014
to EUR 5.2 bn in 2024 13). At the same time, as we have already seen,
taxation and grid charges are hitting industry hard, with levies on large
consumers far exceeding the European average despite some support
mechanisms being put in place for the most electricity-intensive com-

panies (such as the Bandlastprivileg measure to reduce grid tariffs).
As a result, German industrial production has suffered in particular
due to rising energy prices in recent years, including electricity, with
electricity-intensive industries being especially affected (Chart 8).
In order to cope with these higher electricity costs than the European
average, Germany has been using European support schemes to reduce
the cost of electricity for businesses, particularly since the Merz go -

vernment took office in May 2025. The main scheme — the Industries-
trompreis14, made possible by the CISAF — has been approved by the
European Commission for the 2026–2028 period. While it is less bene-
ficial than the indirect carbon cost (ICC) offsetting included in electri -
city prices15, it has a broader scope, meaning that businesses ineligible
for the ICC can benefit from it. Other measures complement this targe-
ted support by addressing other components of the electricity bill. As a

result, the electricity tax has been set at the European minimum for
manufacturing sectors, while the gas-storage tax has been abolished.
The government is also subsidising grid fees, which should halve them.
Overall, according to our estimates based on 2024 prices, the final
price of electricity for these industries could fall by around a quarter
(to 116 EUR/MWh for industries with annual consumption of between
70 and 150 GWh, compared with a European average of 123 EUR/MWh)

(Chart 9). Germany’s advantage over Central and Eastern European
countries, where electricity costs are generally already higher, looks
set to increase.

However, even when taking all of the subsidies into account, the price
of electricity in Germany would remain higher than the levels seen in
France, Spain or Portugal (and even more so in the United States and
China).
FISCAL MEASURES: LIMITED ROOM FOR MANOEUVRE
There are a number of limitations to these European schemes. For
the country implementing them, lower electricity costs mean that

its industry becomes more competitive against industries from other
EU countries, but this does very little to address the disadvantage that
it faces compared with the United States and China. Therefore, this
measure will unlikely be enough to counteract the loss of market share
seen vis-à-vis the United States and China. In addition, the support
schemes are restrictive (conditional on investment in the low-carbon
transition) and are transitional. Finally, their cost is partly passed on

to the public purse or to consumers who do not receive this support
(in Germany, the cost of the new energy price support measures is
estimated at EUR 14.3 bn in 2026, or 0.3% of GDP16).
Some European manufacturers have proposed abolishing or reducing
the carbon tax as a solution to getting electricity prices down quickly.
In Germany, this tax accounted for 14% of the electricity price in the
first half of 2025, which is higher than the EU average (11%). However,

this proposal is already covered by the indirect carbon cost offsetting
mechanism for electricity-intensive industries exposed to carbon al -
lowance prices. In addition, while adjustments are being made — no -
tably to slow the rate at which allowances are reduced — it seems
highly unlikely that the scheme will be significantly challenged. It is
one of the cornerstones of Europe’s policy to combat climate change,
which has been effective thus far, as shown by the falling greenhouse

gas emissions from industry. More broadly, the gradual introduction of
a carbon border adjustment mechanism (CBAM) should mitigate com-
petition distortions caused by environmental practices17.
IN GERMANY AND THE REST OF EUROPE, HEAVY INVEST -
MENT IS DRIVING UP ELECTRICITY BILLS IN THE SHORT
TERM
In response to the challenge of the low-carbon transition and indus -
trial competitiveness, European governments must strike a balance
between decarbonising the electricity mix and ensuring a supply of

energy that is flexible enough (particularly from controllable sources)
to guarantee stable electricity systems. The integration of an increa -
sing proportion of intermittent renewable energy sources requires
massive investment in transmission and storage infrastructure (statio-
nary batteries). This expenditure, which places a long-term burden on
grid costs, reduces the scope for support schemes (particularly public
ones) to address competitiveness gaps. In Germany, the cost of the

transition is estimated at between EUR 122 bn, according to the go -
vernment (2.8% of GDP), and EUR 200 bn, according to the DIHK18 (4.6%
of GDP), by 2030–2035. If we take the ECB’s assumption19 that govern-
ments will cover around 17% of these investments, the annual public
16 The Industriestrompreis is estimated at around EUR 1.3 bn per year, the subsidy for network charges is projected to amount to EUR 6.5 bn in 2026, the abolition of the gas sur -

charge around EUR 3.4 bn, and the reduction in the electricity tax nearly EUR 3 bn per year. Furthermore, the ICC had cost EUR 2.3 bn in 2024 for costs incurred in 2023.

expenditure burden of the transition in Germany would be between
0.5 and 0.8 percentage points of GDP by 2030. However, this budgetary
pressure comes at the same time as three other budgetary increases:
rising social expenditure linked to an ageing population; the necessary
increase to defence budgets to meet NATO’s target of 3.5% of GDP by
2029; and the rising cost of debt-servicing20.
Germany has already made significant progress in maximising its use

of renewable energy, putting it at the head of the pack in Europe. The
country generates 20% of Europe’s renewable electricity (solar and
wind) and accounts for 30% of installed storage capacity (BESS) 21, far
ahead of other European countries (ranking third globally). However,
despite this momentum, Germany’s dependence on gas has not yet
decreased. As a matter of fact, it is only partially offsetting the planned
closure of coal-fired power stations and the phasing out of nuclear

reactors. Furthermore, the intermittent nature of solar and wind power
could prolong this dependence in the short and medium term. In order
to ensure that the German electricity system is flexible, new invest -
ments in gas-fired power plants, supported by public subsidies, are
planned22.

SUBSIDIES ARE HELPING TO ADDRESS THE EMERGENCY
CAUSED BY ENERGY CRISES WITHOUT RESTORING THE
INTERNATIONAL COMPETITIVENESS OF ENERGY-INTEN-
SIVE INDUSTRIES
In response to the impact of energy crises on industrial competitive -
ness, European governments are finding themselves having to strike a
delicate balance between providing short-term support to industries

exposed to increased international competition and continuing — if
not, accelerating — energy transition policies which will result in hi -
gher costs for electricity-network users for several years to come.
Beyond direct economic support measures, which are difficult to sus -
tain long term, four priority areas are emerging:
1/ Demonstrating flexibility in implementing European directives in
order to factor in national specificities and the differing timeframes

between energy crises (which can lead to irreversible industrial conse-
quences) and reforms (which have a longer-term horizon). The relaxa-
tion of the European carbon tax mechanism 23 appears to be a step
towards this.
2/ Continuing efforts to decarbonise the electricity mix, which are still
the main vehicle for sustainably reducing electricity prices for Euro -
pean industries in the medium and long term.
3/ Optimising the use of electricity networks on the demand side, in

particular through smart grids, a flagship element of the Commission’s
COM (2026) 600 reform presented last July24.
4/ Coordinating European electricity networks in order to harness the
complementarities between national systems.
Article completed on 19 October 2026

弘塑 3131 TT  ( 電子上游–設備 ) 
 ) 
訂單能見度無虞 
投資與評價 
1. 弘塑 2Q26 毛利率僅達 38.9%， 低於我們原先及市場預期的40%水準，
主要受到添鴻產品結構轉差及新廠折舊攤提影響。 
2. 3Q26 受惠於 CoWoS 等機台認列數成長及強勁拉貨需求，營收達
22.21 億元；研究部預估 EPS 為 15.86 元。 
3. FY26 全年出機量預估維持在 220 至 240 台，展望至 FY27/FY28，
FY27 設備出貨量預估將年增 50%，FY28 亦有機會再成長 50%。 
4. 研究部預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 60.66 元/96.33 元，維持弘塑買進投
資建議，目標價 2,500 元，以 26x FY27 獲利預估。 
重點更新 
至 FY28 出貨量能將持續成長 
研究部認為弘塑設備出貨展望能見度將能延伸至 FY28。 由於晶圓廠 、OSAT、
記憶體客戶皆對先進封裝設備需求相當迫切，設備交期已長達 10 至 12 個
月，惟受限於短期人力供應與廠房籌備進度， FY26 全年出機量預估維持在 
220 至 240 台。展望至 FY27/FY28，FY27 設備出貨量預估將年增 50%，
FY28 亦有機會再成長 50%。 
添鴻化學品業務將於 1Q27 逐漸看見轉機 
隨 OSAT 客戶於今年 Q2/Q3 進入裝機密集期，新產線仍須經測試與量產爬
坡，相關的化學品需求通常會在設備交機後約 3~4 個月逐步提升，因此短期
營收仍受新廠折舊與稼動率偏低影響，預期添鴻的整體稼動率與毛利率有望
在 1Q27 逐漸擺脫谷底，迎來較為明顯的營運轉機。 
訂單能見度無虞 
展望 2026 年至 2028 年 ， 在各先進封裝平台強勁需求下 ， 設備產能已告全
滿，交期長達 10 至 12 個月，訂單能見度直達 2H27。就產品組合來看，
設備業務高階產品比重將逐漸提升，預估至 FY27 SoIC /CPO 等營收占比將
提升至 10~15%，添鴻化學品也將受惠於 OSAT 客戶逐漸提升拉貨動能，整
體營收與獲利成長動能明確。研究部預估 FY26/27 EPS 分別為 60.66 元
/96.33 元(YoY＋33.5%/+58.8%)。弘塑目前交易於 35/22 倍之 2026/2027 
年獲利預估。研究部維持買進投資建議，目標價 2,500 元，以 26x FY27 獲
利預估。

訪談內容 
3Q26 受惠於機台認列數成長，營收呈現季增 
弘塑 2Q26 營收 17.27 億元 (QoQ +8.2% / YoY +5.9%)，符合我們原先預估的 16.88 億元；毛
利率僅達 38.9% (QoQ +5.1ppts / YoY -1.7ppts)，低於我們原先及市場預期的 40%水準，主要
受到添鴻產品結構轉差及新廠折舊攤提影響 ；EPS 為 11.18 元 (QoQ -30.6% / YoY +58.6%)，
略低於我們原先預期的 11.73 元。 
3Q26 受惠於 CoWoS 等機台認列數成長及強勁拉貨需求，營收呈現季增，營收達 22.21 億元
(QoQ +28.7%/YoY+48.7%) ；研究部預估毛利率 略為季增至 40.1% (QoQ + 1.2ppts / YoY 
-0.5ppts)，產品組合持續改善，預估 EPS 為 15.86 元(QoQ +41.9%/ YoY +52.5%)。 
展望 4Q26， 受惠於先進封裝及記憶體等強勁需求的持續出機認列，研究部預期營收將持續季增，
預估 4Q26 營收將達 24.87 億元(QoQ +12.0%/YoY+15.7%)，毛利率將持穩於 40.0% (QoQ 
-0.1ppts / YoY -2.1ppts)。整體而言，預估 FY26 全年營收達 80.32 億元(YoY+23.3%)，毛利率
達 38.5%(YoY-2.6 ppts)，EPS 為 60.66 元(YoY+33.5%)。
